## 0. Executive directive

This plan converts every recommendation in the comprehensive project and research review into one phased implementation program for local AI agents. It is designed to be executed through the repository's `continue-research-goal` v4 recursive relay, with an immutable `multi_step` scope and exactly one bounded worker invocation and at most one bounded AgentJob per generation.

The central scientific directive is:

> Complete the already-authorized audit of the active EqSrc candidate, stop the repeated canonical-choice orbit, prove the general source-natural selector obstruction, classify the minimum lawful extensions, make the ontology regime explicit, define genuine source dynamics, derive operational geometry and matter, derive gravitational closure only from those source inputs, and test the result with source-derived benchmarks before any protected promotion.

The central research-system directive is:

> Make assumptions, candidate identity, failed attempts, reviewer independence, status dimensions, generated freshness, and task budgets machine-visible, while keeping validators, process receipts, generated views, and research workflow success strictly separate from scientific proof.

The plan is successful only when every included work item has a canonical tracked disposition, every recommendation has evidence-backed coverage, all required validation and checkpoint conditions pass, and every unresolved protected decision is reported as an exact human action. Plan completion does not require a positive derivation of GR. A precise no-go theorem, a scoped obstruction, a frozen family, or a human-gated stop may be the scientifically correct outcome.

## 1. Authority and non-authority boundary

This plan is a tracked control and implementation source. It is not a physics proof, canonical ontology, source law, metric, matter-coupling law, stress-energy derivation, Einstein-equation derivation, benchmark result, Gate Chair decision, external endorsement, publication authorization, or completed-derivation authority.

The controlling authority order remains:

1. Registered canonical science sources and protected human decisions.
2. `research_control/program_state.yaml` and the latest handoff it names.
3. `registries/DISTANCE_TO_GR_LEDGER.csv` for persistent burden state.
4. Activated Director decisions, AgentJobs, role executions, completions, claim boundaries, and approvals.
5. This implementation plan as a dependency and task-scope source.
6. Generated views, local goal records, validation receipts, dashboards, wiki notes, PDFs, and HTML as non-authoritative aids.

No work item may infer authority from the plan's recommendation, its place in a phase, a validator PASS, a successful commit, a goal-relay state, a task count, a reviewer title, or the existence of a generated artifact.

## 4. `continue-research-goal` v4 orchestration contract

### 4.1 Launcher use

The local operator must invoke `$continue-research-goal` in the Codex desktop environment only after the exact goal and reasoning effort have been displayed together and unambiguously accepted. The launcher itself performs no research and must create exactly one initial successor discussion.

Recommended exact goal text:

```text
Implement every included work item in implementations_plans/recommendations_implementation_plan_continue_task-v21.md in the exact dependency order and authority boundaries recorded by that plan. Use the continue-research-goal v4 recursive relay, one bounded worker invocation and at most one bounded AgentJob per generation. Preserve all source authority, claim boundaries, human gates, validation and checkpoint requirements, no-progress and repeated-state stops, repository identity, and no-scope-expansion rules. Complete, precisely obstruct, freeze, defer at a human gate, supersede, or mark conditionally not required every included work item using canonical tracked evidence, then produce the final v21 recommendation coverage, gate-consistency, provenance, no-overclaim, and status synthesis.
```

The operator must then display and confirm:

```text
reasoning_effort: "max"
```

A different reasoning effort is lawful only if it is explicitly selected, supported by the active task-tool model, and confirmed together with the exact goal before initialization.

### 4.2 Recommended scheduling guards

```yaml
max_continue_passes: null
deadline_at: null
fixed_guards:
  max_repeated_state_fingerprints: 1
  max_live_continuations: 1
  handoff_ready_timeout_seconds: 60
  stop_on_human_gate: true
  stop_on_validation_failure: true
  stop_on_checkpoint_failure: true
  stop_on_unexpected_dirty_state: true
  stop_on_no_progress: true
  stop_on_repeated_state: true
  stop_on_capability_loss: true
  stop_on_branch_or_repository_mismatch: true
```

`null` disables only the pass-count or deadline horizon. It does not weaken any fixed guard, one-AgentJob boundary, protected authority, scope contract, validation, checkpoint, lease, branch, or no-progress requirement.

```yaml
    path: implementations_plans/recommendations_implementation_plan_continue_task-v21.md
    sha256: <compute from exact tracked bytes at launch>
  exclusions:
    - "No work item outside the 122 IDs in this plan."
    - "No canonical ontology edit without the exact human-gated work item and approval."
    - "No source-law, metric, matter-coupling, Einstein-equation, benchmark, or completed-derivation promotion by workflow status."
    - "No push, merge, rebase, pull request, external publication, reviewer contact, or public outreach without explicit authorization."
    - "No use of ignored goal files, local caches, generated views, validators, registries, role identity, or task status as scientific proof."
    - "No scope expansion, task repurposing, duplicate successor, concurrent relay, or rerun of a consumed generation."
    - "No private chain of thought, credentials, tokens, keys, or private prompts in tracked attempt or review records."
  source_hashes:
    implementations_plans/recommendations_implementation_plan_continue_task-v21.md: <sha256>
    .codex/skills/continue-research-goal/SKILL.md: <sha256>
    .codex/skills/continue-research-goal/references/goal-file-schema.md: <sha256>
    .codex/skills/continue-research-continue-goal/SKILL.md: <sha256>
    .codex/skills/continue-research/SKILL.md: <sha256>
    AGENTS.md: <sha256>
    research_control/AGENTS.md: <sha256>
    research_control/program_state.yaml: <sha256>
    research_control/handoffs/handoff-0772.yaml: <sha256 or updated latest-handoff hash if launch occurs later>
    registries/DISTANCE_TO_GR_LEDGER.csv: <sha256>
  allow_scope_expansion: false
```

If any source hash or the active handoff changes before launch, regenerate the manifest and repeat the combined confirmation. A changed plan or work-item set requires a new goal record.

### 4.4 Worker-skill routing

- Initial generation route: `continue-research`, as required by v4.
- Scientific, mathematical, philosophical, literature, benchmark, review, and integration work items use `continue-research`.
- Project-system implementation work items use `improve-project-system` only through a route approved by canonical sidecar, repair, or exact active AgentJob boundary. A relay frame must not improvise this transition.
- A science completion that discovers a concrete system blocker must emit a registered `project_improvement_signal` and sidecar while preserving the normal research handoff.
- An `improve-project-system` frame consumes exactly one bounded system AgentJob and must use the exact approved dirty-state or repair manifest required by the v4 route.
- Human-gated work items invoke neither worker skill until explicit authorization exists. They may be marked `deferred_human_gate` while dependency-independent included work remains.
- If the relay runtime cannot lawfully route a planned system item under the existing v4 contract, it must terminalize with the exact human action needed to launch a separate system-specific `continue-research-goal` scope. It must not bypass the route contract.

### 4.5 Completion contract

The v21 goal is complete only when canonical evidence proves all of the following:

1. Every included work item has one finalized status: `completed`, `precise_obstruction`, `frozen`, `superseded`, `conditionally_not_required`, or an explicitly resolved human-gated result.
2. No required work item remains merely planned, pending, active, indeterminate, or deferred behind unresolved human action.
3. Every V21 recommendation has direct implementation evidence and final audit coverage.
4. Every state-changing task passed its required validation and governed checkpoint.
5. Every candidate and theorem uses immutable identity and source hashes where the relevant v21 system is available.
6. Gates A through E have internally consistent evidence and authority, whether passed, failed, obstructed, or human-denied.
7. Final provenance, freshness, no-target, no-overclaim, and external-independence audits have no unresolved blocking finding.
8. The final synthesis separates science, philosophy, methodology, software, publication, and open human actions.
9. The goal record, generation receipts, leases, successor state, and terminal summary validate under v4.

A positive GR derivation is not required for plan completion. An unresolved protected human gate prevents `terminal_complete`; in that case the relay must end with the deterministic human-action-required terminal.

## 5. Global implementation rules

### 5.1 One work item per generation

Each plan task is one immutable work item. Each executing frame invokes exactly one worker skill and at most one outer AgentJob. No agent may batch work-item IDs because files are adjacent or because a scheduling horizon is unlimited.

### 5.2 Research-control transaction

Every state-changing work item must create the normal task, decision, AgentJob, role execution, completion, documentation-impact, registry, handoff, memory-sync, validation, and checkpoint records required by the current repository contract. Activated records are immutable and must be superseded rather than rewritten.

### 5.3 Scientific status

Every physics completion must name the target derivation milestone and exact burden, include new mathematical payload or a precise obstruction, update the expanded Distance-to-GR matrix, evaluate freeze criteria where applicable, and state forbidden conclusions.

### 5.4 Human gates

Canonical ontology adoption, ontology edits, source-law adoption, unscoped metric status, physical matter coupling, Einstein-equation status, benchmark promotion, completed derivation, external submission, authorship, outreach, and reviewer contact remain protected. The plan schedules those questions but does not answer them.

### 5.5 No-target and positive provenance

Passing a target-import checker establishes only a bounded source-purity result. Physical readiness additionally requires provenance, uniqueness or quotient status, dynamics, operational meaning, robustness, and appropriate independent review.

### 5.6 Negative results

A finite countermodel, scoped obstruction, family freeze, no-natural-selector theorem, minimum-extension theorem, or failed benchmark is a lawful scientific result. It must be preserved with exact scope and must not be inflated into global theory rejection unless a separate theorem proves that conclusion.

### 5.7 External sources

External literature and data tasks must prefer primary sources, cite them in APA 7 form, distinguish established results from new project constructions, respect copyright and licenses, and remain draft/control until normal audit and gate sequences are complete.

### 5.8 Project-system separation

System tasks may change schemas, validators, metrics, CI, generated views, packaging, documentation, or data models. They may not edit canonical science or change physics status unless a separately authorized science task and protected authority permit it.

### 5.9 Checkpoint and push

Use `scripts/research_control/checkpoint_research_transaction.py` only after successful state-changing work. It owns final staged acceptance and commit. Push, merge, rebase, or pull-request creation is outside this plan unless explicitly requested later.

## 6. Gate A through Gate E readiness model

| Gate | Purpose | Minimum evidence | Blocked overread |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gate A | Source theory readiness | Ontology regime decided; source objects and morphisms defined; source dynamics explicit; EqSrc or quotient status established; admissible variations, observables, automorphism analysis, audit, stress, and independent review complete. | No metric or downstream promotion. |
| Gate B | Effective geometry readiness | Causal or characteristic structure derived; conformal structure justified; scale operationally calibrated; Lorentzian signature, nondegeneracy, transformation law, uniqueness up to gauge, robustness, and propagation meaning established. | No matter coupling or field equations. |
| Gate C | Matter-coupling readiness | Source matter ontology and dynamics defined; operational devices constructed; common propagation geometry proved; universal coupling, matter action, stress-energy, conservation, equivalence-principle status, audit, stress, and independent review complete. | No Einstein equations or benchmark promotion. |
| Gate D | Einstein-sector readiness | One closure route selected; assumptions satisfied; effective gravitational action or constraint structure derived; field equations obtained; constraints, modes, hyperbolicity, stability, corrections, audit, and independent review complete. | No benchmark or completed-derivation promotion. |
| Gate E | Benchmark and completed-derivation readiness | Source-only benchmark protocol enforced; principal benchmark cases completed; no target input or hidden tuning; independent replication complete; all prior gates valid; assumptions and corrections disclosed. | Requires protected human verdict. |

Each Gate Chair task must decide status dimensions separately. A narrow adoption does not automatically satisfy the next gate.

## 7. Target scientific architecture

The desired dependency structure is:

```text
explicit source ontology regime
  -> source category, automorphisms, EqSrc quotient or obstruction
  -> source state space, dynamics, constraints, observables, phases
  -> controlled reconstruction and causal or characteristic structure
  -> conformal geometry, scale, signature, covariance, operational metric
  -> source matter, operational devices, common cone, universal coupling
  -> matter action, stress-energy, conservation
  -> gravitational closure, effective action, field equations, modes
  -> source-only benchmark suite and independent replication
  -> protected Gate E verdict
```

The graph must not be shortcut by reusing the exact-GR benchmark action, target metric, target solution, target observable, detector semantics, validator status, or process authority as a source premise.

## 8. Target research-operating-system architecture

```text
append-only task and attempt events
  + normalized task taxonomy
  + immutable candidate and theorem lineage
  + multidimensional scientific status
  + assumption ledger
  + blind and independence-classified reviews
  + proof-object registry
  + semantic-smuggling fixtures
  + physics payload and family freeze gates
  + separate science and system budgets
  -> generated registries, frontiers, graphs, dashboards, and publication packages
```

Generated views remain derivatives. Registered science sources and protected decisions remain scientific authority.

## 10. Phase order and dependency policy

```yaml
phase_order:
  - P0: "V21 intake, immutable relay scope, registration, and baseline freeze"
  - P1: "Active EqSrc audit, candidate-family consolidation, and route-orbit stop"
  - P2: "General source-natural selector theorem"
  - P3: "Minimal extension classification and quotient-based EqSrc"
  - P4: "Ontology regime decision and assumption closure"
  - P5: "Closed source theory and source dynamics"
  - P6: "Causal, conformal, and effective metric reconstruction"
  - P7: "Source matter, operational semantics, and universal coupling"
  - P8: "Gravitational closure and Einstein-sector derivation"
  - P9: "Source-derived benchmark suite and independent replication"
  - P10: "Canonical research data model, lineage, and event history"
  - P11: "Independent review, mechanized proof, and scientific QA"
  - P12: "Research governance, budgets, freeze policy, and quality metrics"
  - P13: "Software, CI, reproducibility, and freshness modernization"
  - P14: "Philosophical claim architecture and public status semantics"
  - P15: "Publication, reproducibility, and external-engagement program"
  - P16: "Final integration, recommendation coverage, and goal closure"
```

The dependency graph, not phase numbering alone, controls execution. P10 through P13 and portions of P14 and P15 are deliberately dependency-independent of protected scientific gates so the relay may continue useful included work while a human decision is deferred. No task may begin until every listed dependency has a qualifying finalized status.

## 11. Standard work-item completion record

Every v21 completion must contain fields equivalent to:

```yaml
implementation_plan_receipt:
  plan_id: "recommendations_implementation_plan_continue_task-v21"
  plan_path: "implementations_plans/recommendations_implementation_plan_continue_task-v21.md"
  plan_task_id: "P<phase>-T<nn>"
  recommendation_ids: ["V21-Rnn"]
  dependency_source_sha256: string
  scope_contract_sha256: string

work_item_result:
  work_item_status: completed | precise_obstruction | frozen | superseded | conditionally_not_required | deferred_human_gate | blocked
  worker_skill: continue-research | improve-project-system | none_human_gate
  task_id: string
  agent_job_id: string
  completion_path: string
  completion_sha256: string
  checkpoint_commit: string
  out_of_scope_remaining_work: [string]

scientific_status:
  target_derivation_milestone: string
  milestone_burden: string
  distance_to_gr_delta:
    changed: boolean
    effect: string
    ledger_row_updated: boolean
  assumption_delta: [string]
  candidate_ids: [string]
  theorem_statement_hashes: [string]
  new_mathematical_payload: [object]
  physics_promotion_authorized: false

authority_boundaries:
  proof_authority: boolean
  canonical_ontology_edit_authorized: boolean
  source_law_adoption_authorized: boolean
  metric_adoption_authorized: boolean
  matter_coupling_adoption_authorized: boolean
  einstein_equation_adoption_authorized: boolean
  benchmark_promotion_authorized: boolean
  completed_derivation_authorized: boolean
  authority_source_paths: [string]

validation_evidence:
  selected_gate_ids: [string]
  statuses: {gate_id: status}
  compact_receipt_paths: [string]
  compact_receipt_hashes: [string]
  staged_tree_hash: string
  git_diff_check: PASS | FAIL

recommendation_coverage:
  implementation_status: completed | blocked | deferred | superseded | conditionally_not_required
  coverage_effect: direct | scientific_validation | system_validation | audit | publication | measurement
```

## 13. Phase exit criteria

### P0: V21 intake, immutable relay scope, registration, and baseline freeze

- Objective: Register the complete v21 plan, materialize an exact dependency graph and continue-research-goal v4 multi-step scope, and freeze the starting scientific and project-system evidence.
- Work items: 5.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P1-T01`, `P10-T01`, `P11-T01`, `P13-T01`, `P13-T03`, `P14-T01`.

### P1: Active EqSrc audit, candidate-family consolidation, and route-orbit stop

- Objective: Complete the already-authorized active audit, consolidate the recent EqSrc candidate lineage, and prevent another representation-level selector loop without a qualifying theorem or source-law delta.
- Work items: 5.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P2-T01`.

### P2: General source-natural selector theorem

- Objective: Define the source category and prove, mechanize, audit, stress, and independently review the general fixed-point obstruction for canonical source selection.
- Work items: 8.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P11-T03`, `P14-T05`, `P15-T03`, `P3-T01`.

### P3: Minimal extension classification and quotient-based EqSrc

- Objective: Classify the lawful responses to selector obstruction, redesign EqSrc around quotient or invariant structure, and freeze superseded representative-selection families.
- Work items: 7.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P14-T05`, `P15-T03`, `P4-T01`.

### P4: Ontology regime decision and assumption closure

- Objective: Make the continuum-first versus emergence-first choice explicit under protected authority, close the assumption ledger, and remove the current hybrid ambiguity.
- Work items: 6.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Human-gated items may be deferred only while dependency-independent included work remains. Dependent science stays blocked.
- Cross-phase successors: `P14-T04`, `P5-T01`.

### P5: Closed source theory and source dynamics

- Objective: Define a compact source theory, fix Phi_lambda semantics, construct dynamics, and establish locality, constraints, conserved structure, stable phases, perturbations, and coarse-graining.
- Work items: 8.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P14-T04`, `P6-T01`.

### P6: Causal, conformal, and effective metric reconstruction

- Objective: Derive physical causal structure first, then scale, signature, nondegeneracy, covariance, uniqueness, and an honestly typed effective geometry.
- Work items: 8.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P15-T04`, `P7-T01`.

### P7: Source matter, operational semantics, and universal coupling

- Objective: Define matter at source level, operational clocks and detectors, common propagation geometry, matter action, stress-energy, conservation, and equivalence-principle status.
- Work items: 8.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Human-gated items may be deferred only while dependency-independent included work remains. Dependent science stays blocked.
- Cross-phase successors: `P8-T01`.

### P8: Gravitational closure and Einstein-sector derivation

- Objective: Select one closure route, derive the effective gravitational action and field equations, and classify constraints, modes, corrections, and stability.
- Work items: 7.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Human-gated items may be deferred only while dependency-independent included work remains. Dependent science stays blocked.
- Cross-phase successors: `P9-T01`.

### P9: Source-derived benchmark suite and independent replication

- Objective: Recover the principal GR benchmarks from source inputs only, perform full no-target audit, and obtain independent replication before any benchmark or completion verdict.
- Work items: 9.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Human-gated items may be deferred only while dependency-independent included work remains. Dependent science stays blocked.
- Cross-phase successors: `P15-T05`, `P16-T01`.

### P10: Canonical research data model, lineage, and event history

- Objective: Normalize task and scientific status data, add candidate and attempt lineage, and move toward an append-only canonical event model with generated views.
- Work items: 9.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P11-T01`, `P11-T03`, `P11-T05`, `P12-T01`, `P12-T02`, `P12-T03`, `P12-T05`, `P12-T06`, `P13-T04`, `P13-T07`, `P14-T01`, `P16-T01`, `P16-T03`.

### P11: Independent review, mechanized proof, and scientific QA

- Objective: Implement blind and diverse review, proof-assistant support, semantic-smuggling red teams, positive-provenance gates, and reviewer-agreement evidence.
- Work items: 8.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P12-T05`, `P12-T07`, `P13-T05`, `P14-T03`, `P16-T01`.

### P12: Research governance, budgets, freeze policy, and quality metrics

- Objective: Add payload and freeze gates, separate physics and project-system budgets, expose rework, and measure durable scientific quality rather than packet volume.
- Work items: 7.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P15-T02`, `P16-T01`.

### P13: Software, CI, reproducibility, and freshness modernization

- Objective: Complete validation-planner cutover, modernize packaging and test quality, provide reproducible environments, enforce generated freshness, and clarify checker authority.
- Work items: 8.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: `P15-T02`, `P16-T01`, `P16-T03`.

### P14: Philosophical claim architecture and public status semantics

- Objective: Separate kinds of success, define ontology success criteria, decide the status of the Æflow congruence, and align public language with positive-provenance requirements.
- Work items: 6.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Human-gated items may be deferred only while dependency-independent included work remains. Dependent science stays blocked.
- Cross-phase successors: `P15-T01`, `P15-T03`, `P16-T01`.

### P15: Publication, reproducibility, and external-engagement program

- Objective: Prepare separate publication products and reproducibility packages, obtain actual external review, and prohibit premature derivation publication.
- Work items: 7.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Human-gated items may be deferred only while dependency-independent included work remains. Dependent science stays blocked.
- Cross-phase successors: `P16-T01`, `P16-T03`.

### P16: Final integration, recommendation coverage, and goal closure

- Objective: Audit every recommendation, dependency, gate, claim boundary, source hash, and completion criterion, then close or human-gate the v21 relay with an exact next route.
- Work items: 6.
- Exit requires every non-human-gated work item in the phase to have a qualifying finalized status and every phase validator to pass.
- Cross-phase successors: none; this phase contributes to final integration.

## 14. Standard task execution protocol

1. Validate the goal record, lease parity, work-item ID, immutable route, repository binding, current reasoning effort, and predecessor handoff before claiming a generation.
2. Run the current memory preflight and at least one targeted retrieval query, then inspect the canonical sources behind every hit that affects the task.
3. Resolve `continue_research.py --summary`, the active task and AgentJob, latest handoff, current ledger, and relevant registries.
4. Confirm every dependency has a qualifying finalized status and that the task is included in the immutable scope.
5. Select the narrowest registered role or task overlay and preserve parent-child synthesis for physics work.
6. Define the task's exact claim boundary, source allowlist, write allowlist, expected payload, validators, and stop conditions before execution.
7. Consume the generation exactly once, invoke the approved worker exactly once, and execute at most one outer AgentJob.
8. Write durable source artifacts, task records, completion evidence, recommendation coverage, and handoff state.
9. Run affected validation, generated-system synchronization, documentation-impact and project-improvement-signal checks, research-control diff, focused tests, and `git diff --check`.
10. Use the governed checkpoint for final staged acceptance and commit. Never push unless separately requested.
11. After return, verify canonical repository state directly, record the exact work result and fingerprint, and choose completion, a distinct recovery strategy, lawful continuation, or protected stop.
12. Never rerun the same consumed generation or the same recovery strategy for the same blocker.

#### Objective

Register this Markdown plan as the canonical draft/control dependency source for the v21 implementation program without changing scientific claim status.

#### Preconditions and dependency evidence

- This is a root work item in the immutable scope.
- The goal record, discussion contract, scope contract, generation route, lease parity, repository root, branch, HEAD, and current reasoning effort must validate before generation claim.
- The active research or project-system handoff must permit this exact work item or a tracked decision must lawfully select it.
- The working tree must be clean or match the exact approved repair manifest.

#### Required source inspection

- `AGENTS.md`
- `research_control/AGENTS.md`
- `research_control/program_state.yaml`
- `research_control/current_frontier.md`
- `the latest tracked handoff named by program_state.yaml`
- `registries/DISTANCE_TO_GR_LEDGER.csv`
- `registries/RESEARCH_TASK_REGISTRY.csv`
- `registries/AGENT_JOB_REGISTRY.csv`
- `implementations_plans/recommendations_implementation_plan_continue_task-v19.md`
- `.codex/skills/continue-research-goal/references/goal-file-schema.md`
- `research_control/design/gr_derivation_burden_map.md`

- No unrelated tracked change remains.
- The canonical after-state fingerprint is new or the work item has a lawful no-progress, obstruction, or human-gate disposition.

#### Stop, repair, and rollback conditions

- Stop if redistribution rights or privacy cannot be established.
- Stop on repository or branch mismatch, lease ambiguity, unsupported reasoning effort, unknown consumed invocation, unexpected dirty state, validation failure, checkpoint failure, repeated fingerprint, or no progress without a distinct safe strategy.
- Never rerun a consumed generation or repeat the same recovery strategy for the same blocker.
- Preserve failed artifacts and receipts. Roll back only through the current governed transaction or an exact subsequent repair task.

## 17. Global success measures

### 17.1 Scientific measures

- Number of prior selector obstructions subsumed by a general theorem.
- Reduction in untracked or hidden assumptions per promoted scientific object.
- Number of source-derived structures that survive blind, independent, and mechanized review.
- Existence of explicit source dynamics and nontrivial solutions.
- Gate B through Gate D readiness dimensions satisfied by source-derived evidence.
- Benchmark cases recovered without target premises and with independent reproduction.
- Durable theorem, obstruction, or ledger deltas rather than task volume.

### 17.2 Research-system measures

- Fraction of new tasks using normalized taxonomy and immutable candidate identity.
- Visibility of failed attempts, repairs, and abandoned routes.
- Rate of repeated candidate-family routing after freeze policy.
- Physics-bearing versus project-system budget balance.
- Reviewer disagreement by independence class.
- Semantic-smuggling fault-detection rate on adversarial fixtures.
- Generated-view freshness and historical-readability parity.
- Validation-planner false-negative and hard-mismatch rate after cutover.

All measures are diagnostic. No metric, score, count, dashboard, or trend changes scientific truth or protected authority.

## 18. Global rollback and recovery policy

- Every state-changing task uses the governed checkpoint, which stages only authorized paths and preserves entry-index restoration.
- A failed science candidate is preserved and frozen or repaired in a new task; it is not silently edited into a PASS.
- A failed system migration retains the previous authoritative path and records exact mismatch evidence.
- Event-store, taxonomy, path, proof, and CI migrations remain reversible until parity and non-regression are proven.
- Human-gated tasks are never recovered by an AI-selected substitute decision.
- An ambiguous task dispatch, duplicate child, uncertain invocation return, or corrupt goal state terminates with exact human intervention under the v4 schema.
- Unexpected scope requirements require a new human-confirmed goal, never an amendment to the immutable scope contract.

## 19. Final forbidden-conclusion statement

Nothing in this implementation plan, its registration, the continue-research-goal relay, a completed work item, a proof-assistant build, a validator PASS, a candidate surviving stress, a project-system migration, an internal red-team review, a generated dashboard, a benchmark script, a commit, or a publication draft by itself authorizes:

- canonical ontology edit or ontology adoption;
- source-law adoption;
- general EqSrc discharge;
- RetainH or GenH adoption;
- unscoped effective metric or Lorentzian metric status;
- physical detector semantics;
- universal matter coupling;
- matter action or stress-energy adoption;
- Einstein-equation derivation;
- exact-GR benchmark promotion;
- Gate Chair verdict;
- external endorsement or independent replication;
- future source-extension impossibility or global theory rejection;
- external submission, outreach, or authorship action;
- completed derivation.

Only the exact canonical evidence and protected authority named by the relevant work item can establish its bounded result.

## 20. Final handoff rule

After P16-T06, the relay must do exactly one of the following:

1. Use `terminal_complete` when every completion criterion is canonically proved, release both leases, create no successor, and render the deterministic summary beginning `Goal reached.` and ending `That goal was reached.`
2. Continue to one distinct included repair strategy when a new canonical fingerprint and lawful route exist.
3. Terminalize with `Goal not reached - human action required` when a protected decision, publication action, external reviewer, repository or capability repair, or other exact human intervention is the sole remaining route.

The relay must never create work outside the immutable v21 work-item ledger.
